\displaystyle+C|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|w^{-1}|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla w_{t}|_{2}|\nabla w_{t}|_{4}|l_{tt}|_{4},

\displaystyle I_{20}=

\displaystyle-a_{4}\int\nabla h^{\frac{1}{2}}\cdot\nabla l_{tt}l_{tt}+\frac{1}{2}\int(w^{-\nu}h^{-\frac{1}{2}})_{t}|l_{tt}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}|_{2}+|\varphi|_{\infty}|h_{t}|_{\infty}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|w_{t}|_{4}|l_{tt}|_{4})|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2},

in which,
H^{t}(v)
is given by

\displaystyle H^{t}(v)=

\displaystyle 4\alpha\sum_{i=1}^{2}\partial_{i}v_{i}\partial_{itt}v_{i}+2\beta\text{div}v\text{div}v_{tt}+2\alpha\sum_{i\neq j}^{2}\partial_{i}v_{j}\partial_{itt}v_{j}

\displaystyle+2\alpha\sum_{i>j}\left(\partial_{itt}v_{j}\partial_{j}v_{i}+\partial_{i}v_{j}\partial_{jtt}v_{i}\right).

To estimate
I_{15}
and
I_{16}
, one needs Lemma
2.1
and

\displaystyle|l_{tt}|_{4}\leq

\displaystyle(|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}\cdot l_{tt}\cdot l_{tt}|_{2})^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\nabla(h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}\cdot l_{tt}\cdot l_{tt})|_{1}^{\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}(|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla h|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}|l_{tt}|_{4}^{\frac{1}{2}}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}|l_{tt}|_{4}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle|l_{tt}|_{4}\leq

\displaystyle C(|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}\nabla h|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle+|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}),

\displaystyle|g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v|_{\infty}\leq

\displaystyle|g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v|_{4}^{\frac{2}{3}}|\nabla^{2}(g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v)|_{2}^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|g^{\frac{5}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v|_{2}^{\frac{1}{3}}|\nabla^{2}(g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v)|_{2}^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle|\nabla(g^{\frac{5}{2}}\nabla v\cdot\nabla v)|_{1}^{\frac{1}{3}}|\nabla^{2}(g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v)|_{2}^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle(|\nabla g|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{2}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{2}+|g\nabla v|_{2}|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}v|_{2})^{\frac{1}{3}}|\nabla^{2}(g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v)|_{2}^{\frac{1}{3}}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c_{4}^{2},

\displaystyle\int ng^{2}H^{t}(v)h^{-\frac{1}{2}}l_{tt}\leq

\displaystyle C|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|v_{tt}|_{2}\big(|n|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}(|\nabla g|_{\infty}+|\psi|_{\infty}|gh^{-1}|_{\infty})

\displaystyle+|\psi|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}-b}|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}\big)|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|n|_{\infty}|v_{tt}|_{2}|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|g^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}v|_{4}|l_{tt}|_{4}

\displaystyle+C|n|_{\infty}|v_{tt}|_{2}|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{4}}|g^{\frac{5}{4}}\nabla v|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}|_{2},

\displaystyle a_{6}\int w\text{div}\psi_{tt}h^{-\frac{1}{2}}l_{tt}=

\displaystyle-a_{6}\int(\nabla wh^{-\frac{1}{2}}+w\nabla h^{-\frac{1}{2}})\cdot\psi_{tt}l_{tt}-a_{6}\int wh^{-\frac{1}{2}}\psi_{tt}\cdot\nabla l_{tt}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\nabla w|_{\infty}+|w^{\frac{\nu}{2}+1}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4}}|\psi|_{\infty})|\psi_{tt}|_{2}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|w|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{4}}|\psi_{tt}|_{2}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}|_{2}.

Multiplying (
5.54
) by
t
and integrating over
(\tau,t)
, then (
5.2
), Lemmas
5.3
-
5.9
and estimates on
I_{i}(i=13,\ldots,20)
imply that

\displaystyle t|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{4}}{4}\int_{\tau}^{t}s|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\text{d}s

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}+M(c_{0})+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{14+3\nu}\int_{\tau}^{t}s|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\text{d}s.

It follows from (
5.47
) and Lemma
2.3
that

\exists s_{k}\ \ \text{s.t.}\ \ s_{k}\longrightarrow 0\quad\text{ and }\quad s_{k}|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(s_{k},x)|_{2}^{2}\longrightarrow 0\quad\text{ as }\quad k\longrightarrow\infty.

Letting
\tau=s_{k}
and
k\rightarrow\infty
in (3.89), then Gronwall’s inequality implies that

t|w^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{4}}{4}\int_{\tau}^{t}s|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\text{d}s+\int_{0}^{t}s|\nabla l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

(5.55)

for
0\leq t\leq T_{7}
, this, together with (
5.51
), gives

t^{\frac{1}{2}}|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|_{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{\frac{\nu}{2}}.

(5.56)

Furthermore, (
5.48
) and (
5.56
) imply

\begin{gathered}|l|_{\infty}=|l_{0}+\int_{0}^{t}l_{s}\text{d}s|_{\infty}\leq|l_{0}|_{\infty}+t|l_{t}|_{\infty}\leq c_{0}+Ct|l_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{2}l_{t}|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq\frac{3}{2}c_{0},\\

for
0\leq t\leq T_{7}
. The proof of Lemma
5.10
is complete.

∎

5.2.5.
Estimates on
u
.

Lemma 5.11
.

For
t\in[0,T_{7}]
, one has

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2})\mathrm{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

(5.57)

\displaystyle|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}^{2}+|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{s}|_{2}^{2})\mathrm{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla u_{s}|_{2}^{2})\mathrm{d}s\leq M(c_{0}).

Proof.

First, multiplying
\eqref{ln}_{2}
by
hu
, integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, using (
5.2
) and Lemmas
5.4
-
5.10
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+a_{2}\alpha|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}h\nabla u|^{2}_{2}+a_{2}(\alpha+\beta)|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}h\text{div}u|^{2}_{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int\big(v\cdot\nabla v+a_{1}\phi\nabla l+l\nabla\phi-a_{2}\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot gQ(v)-a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)\cdot hu

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int h_{t}|u|^{2}-a_{2}\int\nabla(l^{\nu}h^{2})\cdot\hat{Q}(u)\cdot u

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\big(|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|g\nabla v|_{2}(|v|_{\infty}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty})+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi|_{2}|l|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{\infty}|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{2}+|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}

\displaystyle+|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{\infty}|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}h\nabla u|_{2}+|\psi|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}h\nabla u|_{2}\big)|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c_{4}^{4}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|^{2}_{2}+M(c_{0})+\frac{1}{2}a_{2}\alpha|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}h\nabla u|^{2}_{2},

where
\hat{Q}(u)=\alpha\nabla u+(\alpha+\beta)\text{div}u\mathbb{I}_{2}
, together with (
5.1
),

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{\infty}\leq

\displaystyle C|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}|\nabla(h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l)|_{2}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{2}(h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l)|_{2}^{\frac{1}{4}}\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{4\nu+3},

and
Gronwall’s
inequalty implies that for
0\leq t\leq T_{7}
,

\begin{split}&|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+\int^{t}_{0}\big(|\nabla u|^{2}_{2}+|h\nabla u|^{2}_{2}+|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}h\nabla u|^{2}_{2}\big)\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}).\end{split}

(5.58)

Second,

\eqref{ln}_{2}
also implies

\displaystyle l^{-\nu}\left(u_{t}+v\cdot\nabla v+a_{1}\phi\nabla l+l\nabla\phi\right)+a_{2}hLu

(5.59)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle a_{2}gl^{-\nu}\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)+a_{3}\psi\cdot Q(v),

thus
multiplying (
5.59
) by
hu_{t}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}\big(a_{2}\alpha|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+a_{2}(\alpha+\beta)|h\text{div}u|_{2}^{2}\big)+|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int l^{-\nu}\big(v\cdot\nabla v+a_{1}\phi\nabla l+l\nabla\phi-a_{2}g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)-a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)\cdot hu_{t}

\displaystyle+\int a_{2}hh_{t}\big(\alpha|\nabla u|^{2}+(\alpha+\beta)|\text{div}u|^{2}\big)-\int 2a_{2}h\nabla h\cdot\hat{Q}(u)\cdot u_{t}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|v|_{\infty}|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}+|l|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi|_{2}

\displaystyle+|g\nabla v|_{4}|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{4}+|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{2})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}|h\nabla u|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6}|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+M(c_{0})+\frac{1}{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2},

this, together with (

) and Gronwall’s inequality, give that for

,

\displaystyle|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+|\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{s}|_{2}^{2})\mathrm{d}s

(5.60)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})(1+t)\exp\big(M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6}t\big)\leq M(c_{0}).

Since

a_{2}L(h^{\frac{3}{2}}u)=l^{-\nu}h^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{H}-a_{2}G(\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}},u),

(5.61)

where

\displaystyle\mathcal{H}=

\displaystyle-u_{t}-v\cdot\nabla v-l\nabla\phi-a_{1}\phi\nabla l+a_{2}g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)+a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v),

(5.62)

\displaystyle\widetilde{G}=

\displaystyle G(\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}},u)=(\alpha+\beta)\big(\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}}{\rm{div}}u+\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\nabla u+u\cdot\nabla(\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}})\big)

\displaystyle+\alpha\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}}\cdot\nabla u+\alpha{\rm{div}}(u\otimes\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}}).

Thus (

), (

), (

), (

), (

) and Lemmas

-

imply

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{H}|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|v|_{4}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{4}+|l|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}

(5.63)

\displaystyle+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{4}|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{4}+|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{2})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1),

\displaystyle|\widetilde{G}|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C(|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla u|_{2}+|\nabla\psi|_{4}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{4})\leq M(c_{0}),

where one also used

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}\leq|h_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{0}|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}|(h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l)_{t}|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq M(c_{0}),

(5.64)

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{4}\leq|h_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{0}|_{4}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}|(h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l)_{t}|_{4}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle|g\nabla v|_{2}\leq|h_{0}\nabla u_{0}|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}|(g\nabla v)_{t}|_{2}^{2}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle\|l\|_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\leq\|l_{0}\|_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|l_{s}\|^{2}_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\text{d}s\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq M(c_{0}).

Thus (

),(

)-(

,

-

\displaystyle C(|l^{-\nu}h^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathcal{H}|_{2}+|G(\nabla h^{\frac{3}{2}},u)|_{2})

(5.65)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}+1),

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C(|h^{\frac{3}{2}}u|_{D^{2}}+|\nabla\psi|_{4}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{4}+|\psi|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla u|_{2})

\displaystyle C|h^{\frac{3}{2}}u|_{D^{2}}+M(c_{0}),

),(

), yield

.

Next, applying
\partial_{t}
to
(3.1)_{2}
, one has

\displaystyle u_{tt}+a_{2}l^{\nu}hLu_{t}+(v\cdot\nabla v)_{t}+(l\nabla\phi)_{t}+a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{t}

(5.66)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-a_{2}(l^{\nu}h)_{t}Lu+\big(a_{2}g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)+a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)_{t}.

and integrating over

, one has

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+a_{2}\alpha|h\nabla u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+a_{2}(\alpha+\beta)|h\text{div}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\int l^{-\nu}\Big(-(v\cdot\nabla v)_{t}-(l\nabla\phi)_{t}-a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{t}-a_{2}(l^{\nu}h)_{t}Lu

\displaystyle+\big(a_{2}g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)+a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)_{t}\Big)\cdot hu_{t}

\displaystyle-\int 2a_{2}h\nabla h\cdot\hat{Q}(u_{t})\cdot u_{t}+\frac{1}{2}\int(l^{-\nu}h)_{t}|u_{t}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}(|v|_{\infty}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|v_{t}|_{2}|g\nabla v|_{\infty})+|l_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi|_{2}

\displaystyle+|l|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi_{t}|_{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}+|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{t}|_{4}|h\nabla^{2}u|_{2}|h^{\frac{3}{4}}u_{t}|_{4}+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|h\nabla^{2}u|_{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{2}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{\infty}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{4}

Integrating it over

Lemma 5.12
.

For
t\in[0,T_{7}]
, one has

\displaystyle\big(|u|_{D^{3}}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}}\big)(t)\leq M(c_{0})c_{3}^{2\nu+3},

(5.72)

\displaystyle|h\nabla u_{t}|_{2}+|u_{t}|_{D^{1}}+\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|u_{s}|_{D^{2}}^{2})\mathrm{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\big(|h\nabla^{2}u_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{D^{4}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{s}|_{2}^{2}\big)\mathrm{d}s\leq M(c_{0}).

Proof.

First, multiplying (
5.66
) by

and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, one has

(5.73)

where

\displaystyle I_{21}=

\displaystyle\int l^{-\nu}\big(-(v\cdot\nabla v)_{t}-(l\nabla\phi)_{t}-a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{t}

\displaystyle-a_{2}(l^{\nu})_{t}hLu-a_{2}l^{\nu}h_{t}Lu\big)\cdot hu_{tt}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}(|v|_{\infty}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|v_{t}|_{2}|g\nabla v|_{\infty})

\displaystyle+|l_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi|_{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}|\phi|_{\infty}+|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}

\displaystyle+|l|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi_{t}|_{2}+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{4}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},

\displaystyle I_{22}=

\displaystyle\int l^{-\nu}\big(a_{2}g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)+a_{3}l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)_{t}\cdot hu_{tt}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}(\nabla l^{\nu})_{t}|_{2}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}+|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}g_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{\nu}|_{4}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{4}.

\displaystyle+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|g^{-1}h|_{\infty}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}(|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|g\nabla v_{t}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{t}|_{2}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}+|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{4}|\psi|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{4})\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},

\displaystyle I_{23}+I_{24}=

\displaystyle-\int 2a_{2}h\nabla h\hat{Q}(u_{t})\cdot u_{tt}+a_{2}\int hh_{t}\big(\alpha|\nabla u_{t}|^{2}+(\alpha+\beta)|\text{div}u_{t}|^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}(|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|h\nabla u_{t}|_{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}+|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|h\nabla u_{t}|_{2}^{2}).

) over
(\tau,t)
, by (

) and Lemmas

-

,

\displaystyle C|h\nabla u_{t}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{2}\int_{0}^{t}|h\nabla u_{s}|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{~d}s+M(c_{0})(c_{4}^{7\nu+6}t+1).

(5.75)

(5.76)

\displaystyle=a_{2}hLu_{t}-a_{2}G(\psi,u_{t})=l^{-\nu}\mathcal{G}-a_{2}G(\psi,u_{t}),

(5.80)

\displaystyle|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}\leq

\displaystyle C(|hu_{t}|_{D^{2}}+|\nabla u_{t}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty}+|\nabla\psi|_{4}|u_{t}|_{4})

It follows from
\eqref{ln}_{2}
that

\displaystyle a_{2}L(h\nabla^{\varsigma}u)=a_{2}h\nabla^{\varsigma}Lu-a_{2}G(\psi,\nabla^{\varsigma}u)=h\nabla^{\varsigma}(h^{-1}l^{-\nu}\mathcal{H})-a_{2}G(\psi,\nabla^{\varsigma}u),

. This, together with (

)-(

)-(

) and Lemmas

(5.81)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M\left(c_{0}\right)(|u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{6\nu+5}).

Finally, (
5.2
), (
5.75
), (
5.80
)-(
5.81
) and Lemma
5.6
imply that

\int_{0}^{T_{7}}(|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|u|_{D^{4}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{t}|_{2}^{2})\mathrm{d}t\leq M(c_{0}).

The proof of Lemma
5.12
is complete.

∎

Lemma 5.13
.

For
t\in\left[0,T_{7}\right]
, one has

\displaystyle t|u_{t}(t)|_{D^{2}}^{2}+t|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|u(t)|_{D^{4}}^{2}(t)

\displaystyle\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6\nu+4},

(5.82)

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}s(|u_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}u_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2}){\rm{d}}s

\displaystyle\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6\nu+4}.

Proof.

Applying
\partial_{t}
to (
5.66
),
multiplying the resulting equation by
l^{-\nu}u_{tt}
and integrating over
\mathbb{R}^{2}
, one has

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+a_{2}\big(\alpha|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+(\alpha+\beta)|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\text{div}u_{tt}|_{2}^{2}\big)=\sum_{i=25}^{28}I_{i},

(5.83)

where

\displaystyle I_{25}=

\displaystyle\int l^{-\nu}\big(-(v\cdot\nabla v)_{tt}-a_{1}(\phi\nabla l)_{tt}-(l\nabla\phi)_{tt}\big)\cdot u_{tt}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|\nabla v_{t}|_{4}|v_{t}|_{4}+|\nabla v|_{\infty}|v_{tt}|_{2}+|v|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{tt}|_{2}+|\phi|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+|\phi_{tt}|_{2}|\nabla l|_{\infty}+|\phi_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}+|l_{t}|_{4}|\nabla\phi_{t}|_{4}

\displaystyle+|l_{tt}|_{4}|\nabla\phi|_{4}+|l|_{\infty}|\nabla\phi_{tt}|_{2})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},

\displaystyle I_{26}=

\displaystyle a_{3}\int l^{-\nu}\big(l^{\nu}\psi\cdot Q(v)\big)_{tt}\cdot u_{tt}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{tt}|_{2}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|l_{tt}|_{4}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{4}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}^{2}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v|_{\infty}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{tt}|_{2}+|\psi_{t}|_{4}|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{4}|\nabla v|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+|\psi|_{\infty}|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{4}|\nabla v_{t}|_{4}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi_{t}|_{4}|\nabla v_{t}|_{4})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},

\displaystyle I_{27}=

\displaystyle a_{2}\int l^{-\nu}\big(g\nabla l^{\nu}\cdot Q(v)\big)_{tt}\cdot u_{tt}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}\big(|l^{\nu-3}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}^{2}|\nabla l|_{\infty}+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|l_{tt}|_{4}|\nabla l|_{4}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}|\nabla l_{t}|_{4}+|l^{\nu-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{t}|_{2}|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}g_{t}|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}(|\nabla l|_{4}|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}g_{t}|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v_{t}|_{2})\big)|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{4}}|h^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{t}|_{4}|g^{\frac{3}{4}}\nabla v_{t}|_{4}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|\nabla l|_{4}|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}g_{tt}|_{4}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v|_{\infty}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|gh^{-1}|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}}|\sqrt{h}\nabla l|_{\infty}|\sqrt{g}\nabla v_{tt}|_{2}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}(|\nabla l|_{\infty}|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}g_{t}|_{\infty}|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|g\nabla v|_{\infty}|\nabla l_{tt}|_{2})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2},

\displaystyle I_{28}=

\displaystyle-a_{2}\int l^{-\nu}\big((hl^{\nu})_{tt}Lu+2(l^{\nu})_{t}hLu_{t}+2h_{t}l^{\nu}Lu_{t}-l^{\nu}\nabla h\cdot\hat{Q}(u_{tt})\big)\cdot u_{tt}

\displaystyle+\frac{1}{2}\int(l^{-\nu})_{t}|u_{tt}|^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}|_{\infty}(|(l^{\nu})_{t}|_{4}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{4}+|l^{\nu-2}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}^{2}|h\nabla^{2}u|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{tt}|_{4}|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{4}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}^{2}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2}

\displaystyle+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{\infty}|\varphi|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{\infty}|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|l^{\nu}|_{\infty}|\psi|_{\infty}|\sqrt{h}\nabla u_{tt}|_{2}|\varphi|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2}})|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}

\displaystyle+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}|u_{tt}|_{4}

\displaystyle+C|l^{-1}|_{\infty}|l_{tt}|_{4}|h\nabla^{2}u|_{2}|u_{tt}|_{4}+C|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}-1}|_{\infty}|l_{t}|_{4}|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}|_{2}|u_{tt}|_{4}.

Multiplying (
5.83
) by
t
and integrating over
(\tau,t)
, then (
5.2
), Lemmas
5.4
-
5.12
and the estimates of
I_{i}(i=25,\ldots,28)
give that

\displaystyle t|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\frac{a_{2}\alpha}{4}\int_{\tau}^{t}s|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{~d}s

(5.84)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\tau|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{tt}(\tau)|_{2}^{2}+M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6\nu+4}(1+t)+M(c_{0})c_{5}^{2\nu+8}\int_{\tau}^{t}s|l^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{~d}s.

It follows from (
5.75
) and Lemma
2.3
that

\exists s_{k}\quad\text{s.t.}\quad s_{k}\longrightarrow 0\quad\text{ and }\quad s_{k}\left|u_{tt}\left(s_{k},x\right)\right|_{2}^{2}\longrightarrow 0\quad\text{ as }\quad k\longrightarrow\infty.

Letting
\tau=s_{k}
and
k\rightarrow\infty
in (
5.84
), then Gronwall’s inequality implies that

t|u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}s|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{~d}s+\int_{0}^{t}s|\nabla u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{~d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6\nu+4},

(5.85)

for
0\leq t\leq T_{7}
.
Moreover, (
5.80
) and (
5.85
) imply

t^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}+t^{\frac{1}{2}}|\nabla^{4}u(t)|_{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{3\nu+2}.

(5.86)

Now,
(
5.4
), (
5.64
), (
5.78
) and Lemmas
5.4
-
5.12
give that

\displaystyle|\mathcal{G}|_{D^{1}}\leq

\displaystyle C\big(|u_{tt}|_{D^{1}}+\|\nabla v\|_{2}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|v|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2}

(5.87)

\displaystyle+\|l\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}\|\phi_{t}\|_{2}+\|l_{t}\|_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}(|\phi|_{\infty}+\|\nabla\phi\|_{2})

\displaystyle+\|l^{\nu-1}\|_{1,\infty}\|l_{t}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{2}}(\|hLu\|_{1}+|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u|_{2})

\displaystyle+(1+|\psi|_{\infty})(1+|\varphi|_{\infty})(|h^{-\frac{1}{2}}h_{t}|_{L^{\infty}}+|h_{t}|_{D^{2}})\|l^{\nu}\|_{1,\infty}\|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}u\|_{1}

\displaystyle+(|g^{-\frac{1}{2}}g_{t}|_{L^{\infty}}+|g_{t}|_{D^{1}})\|\nabla l^{\nu}\|_{2}\|g^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla v\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla l^{\nu}\|_{2}(|\nabla g|_{\infty}|\nabla v_{t}|_{2}+|g\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2})

\displaystyle+(|g\nabla v|_{\infty}+|\nabla g|_{\infty}\|\nabla v\|_{2}+\|g\nabla^{2}v\|_{1})\|l_{t}\|_{D^{1}\cap D^{2}}\|l^{\nu-1}\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}

\displaystyle+\|l^{\nu-1}\|_{1,\infty}\|l_{t}\|_{D^{1}}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}}\|\nabla v\|_{2}

\displaystyle+\|l^{\nu}\|_{1,\infty}\|\psi_{t}\|_{1}\|\nabla v\|_{2}+\|l^{\nu}\|_{1,\infty}\|\psi\|_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1}}\|\nabla v_{t}\|_{1}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})(|\nabla u_{tt}|_{2}+c_{4}^{4\nu+3}|g\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2}+c_{4}^{5\nu+5}|l_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{5\nu+7}),

\displaystyle|\widehat{G}|_{D^{1}}\leq

\displaystyle C(|\psi|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}+|\nabla\psi|_{4}|\nabla u_{t}|_{4}+|\nabla^{2}\psi|_{2}|u_{t}|_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{2\nu+3}).

Thus (
5.77
), (
5.87
) and Lemmas
2.4
,
5.4
-
5.12
imply that for
0\leq t\leq T_{7}
,

\displaystyle|hu_{t}|_{D^{3}}

\displaystyle\leq C|l^{-\nu}\mathcal{G}|_{D^{1}}+C|\widehat{G}|_{D^{1}}

\displaystyle\leq M(c_{0})\big(\|u_{tt}\|_{1}+|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{6\nu+7}(|g\nabla^{2}v_{t}|_{2}+|l_{t}|_{D^{2}}+1)\big),

\displaystyle|h\nabla^{3}u_{t}(t)|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(|hu_{t}|_{D^{3}}+|u_{t}|_{\infty}|\nabla^{2}\psi|_{2}+|\nabla u_{t}|_{4}|\nabla\psi|_{4}+|\nabla^{2}u_{t}|_{2}|\psi|_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C|hu_{t}|_{D^{3}}+M(c_{0})(|u_{t}|_{D^{2}}+c_{4}^{2\nu+3}),

this, together with (
5.72
), (
5.85
)-(
5.86
) and Lemma
5.6
, yield
\eqref{849}_{2}
.

The proof of Lemma
5.13
is complete.

∎

Thus for
0\leq t\leq T_{7}=\min\{T^{*},\big(1+M(c_{0})c_{5}\big)^{-40-10\nu}\}
, Lemmas
5.3
-
5.13
imply

\displaystyle\|(\phi-\epsilon)(t)\|_{L^{p}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}\phi(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|h\nabla\phi_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\|\phi_{t}(t)\|_{2}^{2}+|\phi_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\phi_{ss}\|_{1}^{2}\text{d}s\leq Cc_{4}^{6},

\displaystyle\|\psi(t)\|_{L^{q}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}^{2}\leq M(c_{0}),\quad|\psi_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq Cc_{3}^{4},\quad|h_{t}(t)|_{\infty}^{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{3}^{\frac{3}{2}}c_{4}^{\frac{1}{2}},

\displaystyle|\psi_{t}(t)|_{D^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|\psi_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|h_{ss}|_{4}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{4},

\displaystyle h(t,x)>\frac{1}{2c_{0}},\quad\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{-2\iota}<\varphi<2c_{0},\quad\widetilde{C}^{-1}\leq gh^{-1}(t,x)\leq\widetilde{C},

\displaystyle|\zeta(t)|_{4}^{2}+\|n(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,q}\cap D^{1,4}\cap D^{2}\cap D^{3}}\leq M(c_{0}),\quad|n_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle|n_{t}(t)|^{2}_{\infty}+|\nabla n_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla n_{t}(t)|_{4}^{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{2},\quad|n_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6},

\displaystyle|(l-\bar{l})(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}l|_{2}^{2}+|\nabla^{2}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{3\nu},

\displaystyle|l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{4\nu+2},

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{8\nu+6},

\displaystyle t|l_{t}(t)|_{D^{2}}^{2}+t|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{1}^{\nu},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}s(|l_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),\quad\frac{1}{2}c_{0}^{-1}\leq l(x,t)\leq\frac{3}{2}c_{0},

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla u(t)|_{2}^{2}+\|u(t)\|_{1}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(\|\nabla u\|_{1}^{2}+|u_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle(|u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{t}(|u|_{D^{3}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|u_{s}|_{D^{1}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle(|u_{t}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h\nabla u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{D^{3}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{t}|u_{s}|_{D^{2}}^{2}\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{3}^{4\nu+6},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{D^{4}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0}),

\displaystyle t|u_{t}(t)|_{D^{2}}^{2}+t|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|u(t)|_{D^{4}}^{2}\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6\nu+4},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}s(|u_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}u_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq M(c_{0})c_{4}^{6\nu+4}.

Let

\displaystyle T^{*}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{T,\big(1+M(c_{0})^{36\nu^{3}+104\nu^{2}+102\nu+36}\big)^{-40-10\nu}\right\},\quad c_{1}^{2}=M(c_{0})^{2},

\displaystyle c_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle=c_{3}^{2}=M(c_{0})^{8\nu+7},\quad c_{4}^{2}=M(c_{0})^{24\nu^{2}+45\nu+23},\quad c_{5}^{2}=M(c_{0})^{72\nu^{3}+207\nu^{2}+204\nu+70},

for
0\leq t\leq T^{*}
, one has the following uniform energy estimates with respect to
\epsilon
:

\displaystyle\|(\phi-\epsilon)(t)\|_{L^{p}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{3}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\phi(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}\phi(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq c_{5}^{2},

(5.88)

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\phi_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|h\nabla\phi_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\|\phi_{t}(t)\|_{2}^{2}+|\phi_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\phi_{ss}\|_{1}^{2}\text{d}s\leq c_{5}^{2},

\displaystyle\|\psi(t)\|_{L^{q}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}^{2}\leq c_{1},\quad|\psi_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq c_{4}^{2},\quad|h_{t}(t)|_{\infty}^{2}\leq c_{4}^{2},

\displaystyle|\psi_{t}(t)|_{D^{1}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|\psi_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|h_{ss}|_{4}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{5}^{2},

\displaystyle h(t,x)>\frac{1}{2c_{0}},\frac{2}{3}\epsilon^{-2\iota}<\varphi<2c_{0},\widetilde{C}^{-1}\leq gh^{-1}(t,x)\leq\widetilde{C},

\displaystyle|\zeta(t)|_{4}^{2}+\|n(t)\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}\cap D^{1,q}\cap D^{1,4}\cap D^{2}\cap D^{3}}\leq c_{1},\quad|n_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}\leq c_{4}^{2},

\displaystyle|n_{t}(t)|^{2}_{\infty}+|\nabla n_{t}(t)|^{2}_{2}+|\nabla n_{t}(t)|_{4}^{2}+|n_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq c_{5}^{2},

\displaystyle|(l-\bar{l})(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}l|_{2}^{2}+|\nabla^{2}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle|l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{2}^{2},

(5.89)

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}l(t)|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(|l_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle t|l_{t}(t)|_{D^{2}}^{2}+t|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}\leq c_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}s(|l_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{1}^{2},\quad c_{1}^{-1}\leq l(x,t)\leq c_{1},

\displaystyle|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla u(t)|_{2}^{2}+\|u(t)\|_{1}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}(\|\nabla u\|_{1}^{2}+|u_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{1}^{2},

\displaystyle(|u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{t}|_{2}^{2})(t)

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}(|u|_{D^{3}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|u_{s}|_{D^{1}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{3}^{2},

\displaystyle(|u_{t}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h\nabla u_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{D^{3}}^{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{1}}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{t}|u_{s}|_{D^{2}}^{2}\text{d}s\leq c_{4}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{ss}|_{2}^{2}+|u|_{D^{4}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}u|_{D^{2}}^{2}+|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{s}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{4}^{2},

\displaystyle t|u_{t}(t)|_{D^{2}}^{2}+t|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|u_{tt}(t)|_{2}^{2}+t|u(t)|_{D^{4}}^{2}\leq c_{5}^{2},

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}s(|u_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}u_{s}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{ss}|_{D^{1}}^{2})\text{d}s\leq c_{5}^{2}.

In fact, we can reset

\displaystyle T^{*}

\displaystyle=\min\left\{T,\Big(1+10\big(10^{6}M(c_{0})\big)^{36\nu^{3}+104\nu^{2}+102\nu+36}\Big)^{-40-10\nu}\right\},

\displaystyle c_{1}^{2}

\displaystyle=10^{2}M(c_{0})^{2},\quad c_{2}^{2}=c_{3}^{2}=10^{2}\big(10^{2}M(c_{0})\big)^{8\nu+7},

\displaystyle c_{4}^{2}

\displaystyle=10^{2}\big(10^{4}M(c_{0})\big)^{24\nu^{2}+45\nu+23},\quad c_{5}^{2}=10^{2}\big(10^{6}M(c_{0})\big)^{72\nu^{3}+207\nu^{2}+204\nu+70},

which ensures that (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) holds with the new settings and leads to the following desired estimates:

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(\|\nabla h\|^{2}_{L^{\infty}\cap L^{q}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}+|\nabla h^{\frac{1}{2}}|_{4}^{2})(t)\leq c_{1}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\inf_{[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}}l(t,x)\geq c^{-1}_{1},\quad\inf_{[0,T^{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}}h(t,x)\geq c_{1}^{-1},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(|l|^{2}_{\infty}+|u|_{\infty}^{2})(t)+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}(|u|^{2}_{D^{2}}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}t\leq c_{1}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(\|u\|^{2}_{1}+|h\nabla u|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l|_{2}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{T^{*}}(|l_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}t\leq c_{2}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(|l_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{2}l|_{2}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{T^{*}}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2})\text{d}t\leq c_{2}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|h\nabla^{3}l|_{2}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{T^{*}}(|l_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{t}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}t\leq c_{2}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\text{ess}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}t(|h^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{2}l_{t}|_{2}^{2})(t)+\int_{0}^{T^{*}}t|h^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}|_{D^{1}}^{2}\text{d}t\leq c_{2}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(|u|^{2}_{D^{2}}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{2}u|^{2}_{2})(t)+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}(|u|_{D^{3}}^{2}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{D^{1}})\text{d}t\leq c_{3}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(|u|^{2}_{D^{3}}+|u_{t}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|h\nabla u_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|h_{t}|^{2}_{D^{1}})(t)+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}(|u|^{2}_{D^{4}}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{D^{2}}+|u_{tt}|^{2}_{2})\text{d}t\leq c_{4}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}(|h^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u|^{2}_{2}+|h_{t}|^{2}_{\infty})(t)+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}(|(h\nabla^{2}u)_{t}|^{2}_{2}+|h\nabla^{4}u|^{2}_{2})\text{d}t\leq c_{4}^{2},\qquad

\displaystyle\text{ess}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}t(|u|^{2}_{D^{4}}+|h\nabla^{2}u_{t}|^{2}_{2})(t)+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}(|h_{tt}|_{4}^{2}+|h_{tt}|^{2}_{D^{1}})\text{d}t\leq c^{2}_{5},\qquad

\displaystyle\text{ess}\sup_{0\leq t\leq T^{*}}t|u_{tt}(t)|^{2}_{2}+\int^{T^{*}}_{0}t(|u_{tt}|^{2}_{D^{1}}+|h^{\frac{1}{2}}u_{tt}|_{D^{1}}^{2}+|u_{t}|^{2}_{D^{3}})\text{d}t\leq c_{5}^{2}.\qquad

6.
Local existence in Theorems
1.1

6.1.
The existence without vacuum

This subsection aim at proving the local-in-time existence of the classical solution to the Cauchy problem under
\phi^{\epsilon}_{0}\geq\epsilon
:

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\phi^{\epsilon}_{t}+u^{\epsilon}\cdot\nabla\phi^{\epsilon}+(\gamma-1)\phi^{\epsilon}\text{div}u^{\epsilon}=0,\\[2.0pt]
&u^{\epsilon}_{t}+u^{\epsilon}\cdot\nabla u^{\epsilon}+a_{1}\phi^{\epsilon}\nabla l^{\epsilon}+l^{\epsilon}\nabla\phi^{\epsilon}+a_{2}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}h^{\epsilon}Lu^{\epsilon}\\[2.0pt]
=&a_{2}h^{\epsilon}\nabla(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}\cdot Q(u^{\epsilon})+a_{3}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}\psi^{\epsilon}\cdot Q(u^{\epsilon}),\\[3.0pt]
&l^{\epsilon}_{t}+u^{\epsilon}\cdot\nabla l^{\epsilon}-a_{4}(\phi^{\epsilon})^{2\iota}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}\Delta l^{\epsilon}\\
=&a_{5}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}n^{\epsilon}(\phi^{\epsilon})^{4\iota}H(u^{\epsilon})+a_{6}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu+1}\text{div}\psi^{\epsilon}+\Theta(\phi^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon},\psi^{\epsilon}),\\[3.0pt]
&(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon})|_{t=0}=(\phi^{\epsilon}_{0},u^{\epsilon}_{0},l^{\epsilon}_{0})=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon,u_{0},l_{0})\quad\quad x\in\mathbb{R}^{2},\\[3.0pt]
&(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon})\rightarrow(\epsilon,0,\bar{l})\quad\text{as}\hskip 5.69054pt|x|\rightarrow\infty\quad{\rm for}\quad t\geq 0.\end{aligned}\right.

(6.1)

Theorem 6.1
.

Assume that (
1.12
),
\epsilon>0
, (
3.3
)-(
3.4
) and (
5.1
) hold, where constant
c_{0}>0
independent of
\epsilon
. Then there exist a unique strong solution
(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon})
in
\left[0,T_{*}\right]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
to (
6.1
) satisfying (
4.3
), for some
T_{*}>0
. Moreover, (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) hold for
\left(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon}\right)
with
T^{*}
replaced by
T_{*}
.

Now, we will use iteration scheme to prove Theorem
1.1
.
We start with construct
\left(\phi^{0},u^{0},l^{0}\right)
, which is taken as the unique solution to

\left\{\begin{aligned} &U_{t}+u_{0}\cdot\nabla U=0,\text{ in }(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&Y_{t}-U^{2\iota}\Delta Y=0,\text{ in }(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&Z_{t}-U^{2\iota}\Delta Z=0,\text{ in }(0,\infty)\times\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&(U,Y,Z)|_{t=0}=(\phi_{0}^{\epsilon},u_{0}^{\epsilon},l_{0}^{\epsilon})=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon,u_{0},l_{0})\text{ in }\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&(U,Y,Z)\rightarrow\left(\epsilon,0,\bar{l}\right)\text{ as }|x|\rightarrow\infty\text{ for }t\geq 0.\end{aligned}\right.

(6.2)

Then choose
\bar{T}\in(0,T^{*}]
sufficiently small such that (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) hold for
(\phi^{0},u^{0},l^{0},h^{0},\psi^{0})
with
T^{*}
replaced by
\bar{T}
.

Proof.

First, let
(v,w,g)=\big(u^{0},l^{0},(\phi^{0})^{2\iota}\big)
, and one has a unique solution
(\phi^{1},u^{1},l^{1})
to (
4.1
). By induction, one can construct
(\phi^{k},u^{k},l^{k})
for
k\geq 1
, and obtain
(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1})
by solving

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\phi_{t}^{k+1}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla\phi^{k+1}+(\gamma-1)\phi^{k+1}\text{div}u^{k}=0,\\
&(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}(u_{t}^{k+1}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla u^{k}+a_{1}\phi^{k+1}\nabla l^{k+1}+l^{k+1}\nabla\phi^{k+1})\\
&+a_{2}h^{k+1}Lu^{k+1}=a_{2}(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}h^{k}\nabla(l^{k+1})^{\nu}\cdot Q(u^{k})+a_{3}\psi^{k+1}\cdot Q(u^{k}),\\
&l_{t}^{k+1}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla l^{k+1}-a_{4}h^{k+1}(l^{k})^{\nu}\Delta l^{k+1}\\
=&a_{5}(l^{k})^{\nu}n^{k+1}(h^{k})^{2}H(u^{k})+a_{6}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}\text{div}\psi^{k+1}+\Pi^{k+1},\\
&(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1})|_{t=0}=(\phi_{0}^{\epsilon},u_{0}^{\epsilon},l_{0}^{\epsilon})=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon,u_{0},l_{0})\text{ in }\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1})\longrightarrow(\epsilon,0,\bar{l})\quad\text{ as }\ |x|\rightarrow\infty\ \text{ for }\ t\geq 0,\end{aligned}\right.

(6.3)

where

\Pi^{k+1}=a_{7}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}(h^{k+1})^{-1}\psi^{k+1}\cdot\psi^{k+1}+a_{8}(l^{k})^{\nu}\nabla l^{k+1}\cdot\psi^{k+1}+a_{9}(l^{k})^{\nu-1}h^{k}\nabla l^{k}\cdot\nabla l^{k}.

Moreover,
(\phi^{k+1},u^{k+1},l^{k+1})
satisfies (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) uniformly in

.

Now, we will show the compactness of
\left(\phi^{k},u^{k},l^{k}\right)
. Denote

\displaystyle\bar{f}^{k+1}=f^{k+1}-f^{k},\quad\text{for}\quad f=\phi,u,l,\psi,h,n.

Then (
6.3
) yield

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\bar{\phi}_{t}^{k+1}+u^{k}\cdot\nabla\bar{\phi}^{k+1}+\bar{u}^{k}\cdot\nabla\phi^{k}+(\gamma-1)(\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\text{div}u^{k}+\phi^{k}\text{div}\bar{u}^{k})=0,\\
&(l^{k+1})^{-\nu}\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}+a_{2}h^{k+1}L\bar{u}^{k+1}+a_{2}\bar{h}^{k+1}Lu^{k}=\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bar{\mathbb{U}}_{i}^{k+1},\\
&\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}-a_{4}h^{k+1}(l^{k})^{\nu}\Delta\bar{l}^{k+1}=\sum_{i=1}^{4}\bar{\mathbb{L}}_{i}^{k+1}+\bar{\Pi}^{k+1},\\
&(\bar{\phi}^{k+1},\bar{u}^{k+1},\bar{l}^{k+1})|_{t=0}=(0,0,0)\text{ in }\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&(\bar{\phi}^{k+1},\bar{u}^{k+1},\bar{l}^{k+1})\longrightarrow(0,0,0)\quad\text{ as }\quad|x|\rightarrow\infty\quad\text{ for }\quad t\geq 0,\end{aligned}\right.

(6.4)

where

(\bar{h}^{k+1},\bar{\phi}^{k+1})\in L^{\infty}([0,\bar{T}];H^{3})\ \ \text{and}\ \ \bar{\psi}^{k+1}\in L^{\infty}([0,\bar{T}];H^{2})\ \ \text{ for }\quad k=1,2,\ldots

Proof.

gives

\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}\leq C\big(|\nabla u^{k}|_{\infty}|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+(|\phi^{k}|_{\infty}|\nabla\bar{u}^{k}|_{2}+|\nabla\phi^{k}|_{\infty}|\bar{u}^{k}|_{2})|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}|_{2}\big),

. Now, the classical energy estimates on

\displaystyle+|(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{l}^{k}|_{2}+|\bar{h}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{h}^{k}|_{2}).

Next, applying

to

multiplying the resulting equation by

\displaystyle\mathcal{C}^{k}(t)\big(\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|_{2}^{2}+|\bar{h}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+\|\bar{\psi}^{k+1}\|_{1}^{2}+|\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}

Now, define

\displaystyle\Gamma^{k+1}(t)=

Then it follows from (
6.21
) and Gronwall’s inequality that

\displaystyle\Gamma^{k+1}(t)+\int_{0}^{t}(|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{s}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{k+1}\nabla\bar{u}_{s}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\int_{0}^{t}\widetilde{\sigma}(|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k-1})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{s}^{k}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{k}\nabla\bar{u}_{s}^{k}|_{2}^{2})\text{d}s+(t+t^{\frac{1}{2}})\sigma\Gamma^{k}(t)

\displaystyle+t\sigma\Gamma^{k-1}(t)+t\sigma\Gamma^{k-2}(t)\Big)\exp(C\sigma^{-3}t+C\sigma^{-3}+\widetilde{\sigma}^{-2}t).

Thus choose
\sigma\in(0,\min\{1,a_{4},a_{2}\alpha\}),T_{*}\in(0,\min\{1,\bar{T}\}]
and
\widetilde{\sigma}\in(0,1)
satisfy

\displaystyle C\widetilde{\sigma}\exp(C\sigma^{-3}T_{*}+C\sigma^{-3}+\widetilde{\sigma}^{-2}T_{*})\leq\frac{1}{32},

\displaystyle C\sigma T_{*}^{\frac{1}{2}}\exp(C\sigma^{-3}T_{*}+C\sigma^{-3}+\widetilde{\sigma}^{-2}T_{*})\leq\frac{1}{32}.

One can obtain that

\displaystyle\sum_{k=1}^{\infty}\Big(\Gamma^{k+1}(T_{*})+\int_{0}^{T_{*}}(|(h^{k+1})^{\frac{1}{2}}(l^{k})^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2}+|h^{k+1}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}^{2})\text{~d}t\Big)<\infty,

which, together with (
5.88
)-(
5.89
), implies

\displaystyle\lim_{k\rightarrow\infty}(\|\bar{\phi}^{k+1}\|_{s^{\prime}}+\|\bar{h}^{k+1}\|_{s^{\prime}}+\|\bar{u}^{k+1}\|_{s^{\prime}}+\|\bar{l}^{k+1}\|_{s^{\prime}}+|\bar{u}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2}+|\bar{l}_{t}^{k+1}|_{2})

\displaystyle=0,

for any
s^{\prime}\in[1,3)
. And there has a subsequence (still denoted by
\left(\phi^{k},h^{k},l^{k},u^{k}\right)
) satisfies

\displaystyle(\phi^{k}-\epsilon,h^{k},l^{k}-\bar{l},u^{k})\overset{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];H^{s^{\prime}})}{\longrightarrow}(\phi^{\epsilon}-\epsilon,h^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon}-\bar{l},u^{\epsilon}),

(6.22)

\displaystyle(u_{t}^{k},l_{t}^{k})\overset{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{2})}{\longrightarrow}(u_{t}^{\epsilon},l_{t}^{\epsilon}),

for limit
\left(\phi^{\epsilon},h^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon}\right)
.

Moreover, it follows from (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) that there has a subsequence (still denoted by
(\phi^{k},h^{k},l^{k},u^{k})
) converges to
\left(\phi^{\epsilon},h^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon}\right)
, which also satisfies
the estimates in
(
5.88
)-(
5.89
) except those weighted estimates on
\phi^{\epsilon}
,
u^{\epsilon}
and
l^{\epsilon}
.

We claim that

\displaystyle(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{k},(h^{k})^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u^{k},t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}^{k}

\displaystyle\overset{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{2})}{\rightharpoonup}(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{\epsilon},(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u^{\epsilon},t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{\epsilon})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}^{\epsilon};

\displaystyle t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}^{k}

\displaystyle\overset{L^{2}([0,T_{*}];L^{2})}{\rightharpoonup}t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}^{\epsilon}.

Indeed, since

\displaystyle(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{h^{k}-h^{\epsilon}}{(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}+(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}},\quad(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}}=\frac{(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}}{(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}+(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}}},

\displaystyle(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{-\frac{1}{4}}

\displaystyle=\frac{-((h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}})}{(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}}},

and
h^{k}
and
h^{\epsilon}
have positive lower bounds independent of
k
, one has

\|\big((h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}},(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}},(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{-\frac{1}{4}}\big)\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})}\rightarrow 0,

(6.23)

as
k\rightarrow\infty
. Thus it follows from (
6.22
)-(
6.23
) and (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) for
\left(\phi^{k},h^{k},l^{k},u^{k}\right)
, the estimates for
\left(\phi^{\epsilon},h^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon}\right)
obtained above that

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big((h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{k}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{\epsilon}\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})}+\|\nabla l^{k}-\nabla l^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{2})})T_{*}\rightarrow 0\text{ as }k\rightarrow\infty,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big((h^{k})^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u^{k}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u^{\epsilon}\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})}T_{*}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(h^{k}\nabla^{3}u^{k}-h^{\epsilon}\nabla^{3}u^{\epsilon})\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{2}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})}+\|h^{k}-h^{\epsilon}\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})})T_{*}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(\nabla^{3}u^{k}-\nabla^{3}u^{\epsilon})\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t\rightarrow 0\text{ as }k\rightarrow\infty,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}^{k}-t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{\epsilon})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}^{\epsilon}\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|(h^{k})^{-\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{-\frac{1}{4}})\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})}T_{*}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}l_{tt}^{k}-t^{\frac{1}{2}}l_{tt}^{\epsilon})\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t\rightarrow 0\text{ as }k\rightarrow\infty,

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}^{k}-t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}}\nabla l_{tt}^{\epsilon}\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C\|(h^{k})^{\frac{1}{4}}-(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}})\|_{L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{\infty})}T_{*}

\displaystyle+C\int_{0}^{T_{*}}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(t^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{tt}^{k}-t^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l_{tt}^{\epsilon})\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}t\rightarrow 0\text{ as }k\rightarrow\infty,

for any test function
\mathcal{X}(t,x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}([0,T_{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2})
, which, along with the lower semicontinuity of norms, yield the uniform boundedness of
(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla l^{\epsilon},(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{3}{2}}\nabla^{3}u^{\epsilon},t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{\epsilon})^{-\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}^{\epsilon}
in
L^{\infty}([0,T_{*}];L^{2})
and
t^{\frac{1}{2}}(h^{\epsilon})^{\frac{1}{4}}l_{tt}^{\epsilon}
in
L^{2}([0,T_{*}];L^{2})
with respect to
\epsilon
. Similarly, one can also obtain the other desired weighed estimates. Hence the corresponding weighted estimates for
\left(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon}\right)
in (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) hold also for the limit.

Next we show that
(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon})
is a weak solution in the sense of distributions to (
6.1
). First, multiplying
\eqref{860}_{3}
by any given
\mathcal{X}(t,x)\in C_{c}^{\infty}\left(\left[0,T_{*}\right]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}\right)
on both sides, and integrating over
(0,t)\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
for
t\in\left(0,T_{*}\right]
, one has

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(l^{k+1}\mathcal{X}_{s}-u^{k}\cdot\nabla l^{k+1}\mathcal{X})\text{d}x\text{d}s=\int l^{k+1}\mathcal{X}(t,x)-\int l_{0}\mathcal{X}(0,x)

(6.24)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(a_{4}(l^{k})^{\nu}h^{k+1}\Delta l^{k+1}+a_{5}(l^{k})^{\nu}n^{k+1}(h^{k})^{2}H(u^{k})\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(a_{6}(l^{k})^{\nu+1}\text{div}\psi^{k+1}+\Pi^{k+1}\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}s.

It follows from the uniform estimates obtained above that one can take the limit
k\rightarrow\infty
in (
6.24
) to get

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}(l^{\epsilon}\mathcal{X}_{s}-u^{\epsilon}\cdot\nabla l^{\epsilon}\mathcal{X})\text{d}x\text{d}s=\int l^{\epsilon}\mathcal{X}(t,x)-\int l_{0}\mathcal{X}(0,x)

(6.25)

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(a_{4}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}h^{\epsilon}\Delta l^{\epsilon}+a_{5}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu}n^{\epsilon}(h^{\epsilon})^{2}H(u^{\epsilon})\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}s

\displaystyle-\int_{0}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}^{2}}\big(a_{6}(l^{\epsilon})^{\nu+1}\text{div}\psi^{\epsilon}+\Theta(\phi^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon},\psi^{\epsilon})\big)\mathcal{X}\text{d}x\text{d}s.

Similarly, one can show that (
\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon}
) also satisfies the equations
\eqref{858}_{1}
,
\eqref{858}_{2}
and the initial data in the sense of distributions. Thus,
(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon})
is a weak solution in the sense of distributions to the Cauchy problem (
6.1
).

For the uniqueness. Let
(\phi_{1},u_{1},l_{1})
and
(\phi_{2},u_{2},l_{2})
be two solutions to (
6.1
) that satisfy (
5.88
)-(
5.89
). Denote

\displaystyle\bar{f}=f_{1}-f_{2},\quad\text{for}\quad f=h,\phi,u,l,\psi.

Then (
6.1
) gives

\left\{\begin{aligned} &\bar{\phi}_{t}+u_{1}\cdot\nabla\bar{\phi}+\bar{u}\cdot\nabla\phi_{2}+(\gamma-1)(\bar{\phi}\text{div}u_{1}+\phi_{2}\text{div}\bar{u})=0,\\
&\bar{u}_{t}+u_{1}\cdot\nabla\bar{u}+l_{1}\nabla\bar{\phi}+a_{1}\phi_{1}\nabla\bar{l}+a_{2}l_{1}^{\nu}h_{1}L\bar{u}\\
=&-\bar{u}\cdot\nabla u_{2}-a_{1}\bar{\phi}\nabla l_{2}-\bar{l}\nabla\phi_{2}-a_{2}(l_{1}^{\nu}h_{1}-l_{2}^{\nu}h_{2})Lu_{2}\\
&+a_{2}\big(h_{1}\nabla l_{1}^{\nu}\cdot Q(u_{1})-h_{2}\nabla l_{2}^{\nu}\cdot Q(u_{2})\big)\\
&+a_{3}\big(l_{1}^{\nu}\psi_{1}\cdot Q\left(u_{1}\right)-l_{2}^{\nu}\psi_{2}\cdot Q\left(u_{2}\right)\big),\\
&\bar{l}_{t}+u_{1}\cdot\nabla\bar{l}+\bar{u}\cdot\nabla l_{2}-a_{4}\phi_{1}^{2\iota}l_{1}^{\nu}\Delta\bar{l}\\
=&a_{4}(\phi_{1}^{2\iota}l_{1}^{\nu}\Delta l_{2}-\phi_{2}^{2\iota}l_{2}^{\nu}\Delta l_{2})+a_{5}\big(l_{1}^{\nu}n_{1}\phi_{1}^{4\iota}H(u_{1})-l_{2}^{\nu}n_{2}\phi_{2}^{4\iota}H(u_{2})\big)\\
&+a_{6}(l_{1}^{\nu+1}\text{div}\psi_{1}-l_{2}^{\nu+1}\text{div}\psi_{2})+\Theta(\phi_{1},l_{1},\psi_{1})-\Theta(\phi_{2},l_{2},\psi_{2}),\\
&\bar{h}_{t}+u_{1}\cdot\nabla\bar{h}+\bar{u}\cdot\nabla h_{2}+(\delta-1)(\bar{h}\text{div}u_{1}+h_{2}\text{div}\bar{u})=0,\\
&\bar{\psi}_{t}+\sum_{k=1}^{2}A_{k}(u_{1})\partial_{k}\bar{\psi}+B(u_{1})\bar{\psi}+a\delta(\bar{h}\nabla\text{div}u_{1}+h_{2}\nabla\text{div}\bar{u})\\
=&-\sum_{k=1}^{2}A_{k}(\bar{u})\partial_{k}\psi_{2}-B(\bar{u})\psi_{2},\\
&(\bar{\phi},\bar{u},\bar{l},\bar{h},\bar{\psi})|_{t=0}=(0,0,0,0,0)\quad\text{ in }\quad\mathbb{R}^{2},\\
&(\bar{\phi},\bar{u},\bar{l},\bar{h},\bar{\psi})\longrightarrow(0,0,0,0,0)\quad\text{ as }|x|\rightarrow\infty\quad\text{ for }\quad t\geq 0.\end{aligned}\right.

(6.26)

Set

\displaystyle\Phi(t)=

\displaystyle\|\bar{\phi}\|_{2}^{2}+|\bar{h}|_{2}+\|\bar{\psi}\|_{1}^{2}+|\bar{l}|_{2}+|h_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}l_{1}^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}|_{2}^{2}+|\bar{l}_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|l_{1}^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{u}|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\alpha|h_{1}\nabla\bar{u}|_{2}^{2}+(\alpha+\beta)|h_{1}\text{div}\bar{u}|_{2}^{2}+|l_{1}^{-\frac{\nu}{2}}h_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}\bar{u}_{t}|_{2}^{2},

then one has

\frac{\text{d}}{\text{d}t}\Phi(t)+C(|h_{1}^{\frac{1}{2}}l_{1}^{\frac{\nu}{2}}\nabla\bar{l}_{t}|_{2}^{2}+|h_{1}\nabla\bar{u}_{t}|_{2}^{2})\leq H(t)\Phi(t),

where
H(t)
satisfying

\int_{0}^{t}H(s)\mathrm{d}s\leq C\quad\text{ for }\quad 0\leq t\leq T_{*},

and Gronwall’s inequality implies

\bar{\phi}=\bar{u}=\bar{l}=0,\quad a.e.

thus the solution is unique.

The time-continuity can be obtained as the process in proving Lemma
4.1
, and this complete the proof of Theorem
6.1
.
∎

6.2.
Limit to the case with vacuum.

With (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) at hand, it is time to prove Theorem
3.1
.

Proof.

First, assume that

\phi_{0}^{\epsilon}=\phi_{0}+\epsilon
for all
\epsilon\in(0,1)
,
then

\displaystyle u_{0}=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-\iota}g_{1}^{\epsilon},\quad\nabla u_{0}=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-2\iota}g_{2}^{\epsilon},\quad Lu_{0}=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-3\iota}g_{3}^{\epsilon},

(6.27)

\displaystyle\nabla\big((\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{3\iota}Lu_{0}\big)=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-\iota}g_{4}^{\epsilon},\quad\nabla l_{0}=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-\iota}g_{5}^{\epsilon},

\displaystyle\Delta l_{0}=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-2\iota}g_{6}^{\epsilon},\quad\nabla\big((\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{2\iota}\Delta l_{0}\big)=(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-\iota}g_{7}^{\epsilon},

where

\begin{array}[]{l}g_{1}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-\iota}}{(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-\iota}}g_{1},\quad g_{2}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-2\iota}}{(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-2\iota}}g_{2},\quad g_{3}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-3\iota}}{(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-3\iota}}g_{3},\\
g_{4}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-4\iota}}{(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-4\iota}}(g_{4}-\frac{\epsilon\nabla\phi_{0}^{3\iota}}{\phi_{0}+\epsilon}\phi_{0}^{\iota}Lu_{0}),\quad g_{5}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-\iota}}{(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-\iota}}g_{5},\\
g_{6}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-2\iota}}{\left(\phi_{0}+\epsilon\right)^{-2\iota}}g_{6},\quad g_{7}^{\epsilon}=\frac{\phi_{0}^{-3\iota}}{(\phi_{0}+\epsilon)^{-3\iota}}(g_{7}-\frac{\epsilon\nabla\phi_{0}^{2\iota}}{\phi_{0}+\epsilon}\phi_{0}^{\iota}\Delta l_{0}).\end{array}

According to (
3.3
)-(
3.4
), one has

\displaystyle 2+\epsilon+\bar{l}+\|\phi^{\epsilon}_{0}-\epsilon\|_{L^{p}\cap D^{1}\cap D^{2}}+|\nabla(\phi_{0}^{\epsilon})^{\iota+1}|_{2}+|(\phi_{0}^{\epsilon})^{2\iota}\nabla^{2}\phi_{0}^{\epsilon}|_{2}+\|u^{\epsilon}_{0}\|_{3}

\displaystyle+\|\nabla h^{\epsilon}_{0}\|_{L^{q}\cap D^{1}}+|(h^{\epsilon}_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}\nabla^{3}h^{\epsilon}_{0}|_{2}+|\nabla(h^{\epsilon}_{0})^{\frac{1}{2}}|_{4}+|(h^{\epsilon}_{0})^{-1}|_{\infty}

\displaystyle+\sum_{i=1}^{7}|g^{\epsilon}_{i}|_{2}+\|l^{\epsilon}_{0}-\bar{l}\|_{3}+|(l^{\epsilon}_{0})^{-1}|_{\infty}\leq\bar{c}_{0},

for some constants
\epsilon_{1}>0
and
0<\epsilon<\epsilon_{1}
,
where constant
\bar{c}_{0}>0
is independent of
\epsilon
. Thus for the initial data
(\phi_{0}^{\epsilon},u_{0}^{\epsilon},l_{0}^{\epsilon})
, Theorem
6.1
implies that (
6.1
) has a unique solution
(\phi^{\epsilon},u^{\epsilon},l^{\epsilon})
in
[0,T_{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
satisfying (
5.88
)-(
5.89
) with
c_{0}
replaced by
\bar{c}_{0}
, where
T_{*}
does not depend on
\epsilon
.
Moreover, one has

Lemma 6.2
.

For all
R_{0}>0
,
\epsilon\in(0,1]
, and ball
B_{R}
which centered at origin with radius
R
, there exists a constant
a_{R_{0}}
independent of
\epsilon
satisfying

\phi^{\epsilon}(t,x)\geq a_{R_{0}}>0,\quad\forall(t,x)\in\left[0,T_{*}\right]\times B_{R_{0}}.

The proof of Lemma
6.2
is similar to Lemma 3.9 in
[
37
]
, the detail is omitted for simplicity.

Second, following the process in proving Theorem
6.1
, we can show that Theorem
3.1
holds, i.e.
(\phi,u,l)
is the unique strong solution to (
3.2
) in
[0,T_{*}]\times\mathbb{R}^{2}
.
∎

Proof.

The proof of Theorem
1.1
follows similar process as
[
8
]
, and the details are omitted for simplicity.

∎
